country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1000
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_agrarian_idyll.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_agrarian_idyll } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_agrarian_idyll
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_agrarian_idyll }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_agrarian_idyll } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_agrarian_idyll
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_agrarian_idyll
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_agrarian_idyll
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_agrarian_idyll } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_agrarian_idyll
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_agrarian_idyll }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_agrarian_idyll } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_agrarian_idyll
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_agrarian_idyll
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_agrarian_idyll
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_agrarian_idyll } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_agrarian_idyll
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_agrarian_idyll }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_agrarian_idyll } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_agrarian_idyll

			}
		}

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_agrarian_idyll } }

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }

	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_agrarian_idyll } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1000 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}

		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1002 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

				is_pool_leader = no

				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1036
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_philosopher_king.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_philosopher_king
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_philosopher_king } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_philosopher_king
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_philosopher_king }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_philosopher_king } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_philosopher_king
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_philosopher_king
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_philosopher_king
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_philosopher_king
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_philosopher_king } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_philosopher_king
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_philosopher_king }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_philosopher_king } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_philosopher_king
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_philosopher_king
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_philosopher_king

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }

			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_philosopher_king
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_philosopher_king } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"

			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_philosopher_king
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_philosopher_king } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1036 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)

			has_valid_civic = civic_reanimated_armies
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_reanimated_armies } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_reanimated_armies
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_reanimated_armies }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_reanimated_armies } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_reanimated_armies
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_reanimated_armies
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_reanimated_armies

			has_valid_civic = civic_reanimated_armies
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_reanimated_armies } }

				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }

	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1040 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_shadow_council
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_shadow_council } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_shadow_council
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_shadow_council }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_shadow_council } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_shadow_council
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_shadow_council
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_shadow_council
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_slaver_guilds
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_slaver_guilds
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_slaver_guilds
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_slaver_guilds } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_slaver_guilds
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_slaver_guilds }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_slaver_guilds } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_slaver_guilds
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_slaver_guilds
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_slaver_guilds
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_slaver_guilds
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_slaver_guilds } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1045 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1047
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_toxic_baths.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}

				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_toxic_baths }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_toxic_baths } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_toxic_baths
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_toxic_baths
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_toxic_baths
			}
		}

		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_toxic_baths_individual_machine

		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

			limit = {

					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_corporate_toxic_baths_individual_machine }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_toxic_baths_individual_machine } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_toxic_baths_individual_machine
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_corporate_toxic_baths_individual_machine
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_toxic_baths_individual_machine
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }

				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_dystopian_society
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_dystopian_society
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_dystopian_society
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_dystopian_society } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_dystopian_society
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_dystopian_society }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_dystopian_society } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_dystopian_society
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_dystopian_society
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_dystopian_society
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_dystopian_society
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_dystopian_society } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1052 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1054
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_civic_guided_sapience.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_guided_sapience
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_civic_guided_sapience } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_civic_guided_sapience
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_civic_guided_sapience }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_civic_guided_sapience } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_civic_guided_sapience
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_civic_guided_sapience
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_civic_guided_sapience
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_hedonism
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corporate_hedonism } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_corporate_hedonism
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_corporate_hedonism }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_hedonism } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_hedonism
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_corporate_hedonism
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_hedonism
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_franchising
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_franchising
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_franchising } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_franchising
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_franchising }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_franchising } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_franchising
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_franchising
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_franchising
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_franchising
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_franchising } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1067 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1076
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_private_prospectors.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2

				is_ruler = no

			}

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4

			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_private_prospectors }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_private_prospectors } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_private_prospectors
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_private_prospectors
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_private_prospectors
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_guided_sapience
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_civic_guided_sapience_corperate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_civic_guided_sapience_corperate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_civic_guided_sapience_corperate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_civic_guided_sapience_corperate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_civic_guided_sapience_corperate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_civic_guided_sapience_corperate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_civic_guided_sapience_corperate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}

	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1088

	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no

			}

		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			set_council_position = councilor_psionic_sovereign

		if = {

				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_psionic_sovereign } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_psionic_sovereign
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_psionic_sovereign
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_psionic_sovereign
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_hyperspace_specialty
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_hyperspace_specialty } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_hyperspace_specialty
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_hyperspace_specialty }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_hyperspace_specialty } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_hyperspace_specialty
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_hyperspace_specialty
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_hyperspace_specialty
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_hyperspace_specialty
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_hyperspace_specialty } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1090 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_dark_consortium_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_dark_consortium_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_dark_consortium_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_dark_consortium_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_dark_consortium_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_dark_consortium_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_dark_consortium

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1099
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_augmentation_bazaars.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }

			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_augmentation_bazaars }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_augmentation_bazaars } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_augmentation_bazaars
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_augmentation_bazaars

			}

				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_natural_design } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_natural_design
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_natural_design
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_natural_design
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_natural_design
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_natural_design } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1100 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_corporate_galactic_curators }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_galactic_curators } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_galactic_curators
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_corporate_galactic_curators
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_galactic_curators
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_galactic_curators
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corporate_galactic_curators } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }

		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1111 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1112
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_beastmasters.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_beastmasters
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_beastmasters } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_beastmasters
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_beastmasters }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_beastmasters } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_beastmasters
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_beastmasters
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_beastmasters
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_corporate_beastmasters
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_beastmasters
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_beastmasters
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corporate_beastmasters } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1113 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1114
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_head_treasure_hunter.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_country_flag = unlocks_head_treasure_hunter_council
			is_megacorp = no
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_head_treasure_hunter } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_head_treasure_hunter
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_head_treasure_hunter }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_head_treasure_hunter } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_head_treasure_hunter
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_head_treasure_hunter

			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes

				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_genetic_identification
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_corporate_genetic_identification
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_genetic_identification
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_genetic_identification
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corporate_genetic_identification } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1119

	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

			}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }

				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_head_of_education } }

			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_world_forgers
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_thermal_engineer } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1122 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1123
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_world_forgers
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_world_forgers
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_corporate_thermal_engineer
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {

				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_energy_curator } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1127 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1128
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_chief_medium.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_superstitious_beliefs
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_chief_medium } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_chief_medium
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_chief_medium }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chief_medium } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chief_medium
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_chief_medium
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_chief_medium
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_superstitious_beliefs
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_chief_medium } }
		}

				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_secret_societies
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_secret_societies }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_secret_societies } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_secret_societies
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_secret_societies
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies } }
		}

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1130 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_secret_societies_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_secret_societies_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_secret_societies_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_secret_societies_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_secret_societies_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_secret_societies_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_secret_societies_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_secret_societies_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"

			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

			}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_secret_societies_corporate
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_secret_societies_corporate } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1135 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_test_marketer
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_test_marketer }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_test_marketer } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_test_marketer
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_test_marketer
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_test_marketer
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_twisted_experimenters_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_test_marketer } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_test_marketer
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_test_marketer }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_test_marketer } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_test_marketer
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_test_marketer
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_test_marketer
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_twisted_experimenters_corporate
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_test_marketer } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1137 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1139
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_executive_vessel.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_tankbound_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_executive_vessel } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_executive_vessel
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_executive_vessel }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_executive_vessel } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_executive_vessel
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_executive_vessel
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_executive_vessel
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}

				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_star_seekers
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_star_seekers } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_star_seekers
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_star_seekers }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_star_seekers } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_star_seekers
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_star_seekers
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_star_seekers
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}

		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_hired_guns
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_asset_security_manager } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_asset_security_manager
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_asset_security_manager }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_asset_security_manager } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_asset_security_manager
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_asset_security_manager
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_asset_security_manager
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_hired_guns
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_asset_security_manager } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1147 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_cor_time_honored
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_time_honored } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_time_honored
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_time_honored }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_time_honored } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_time_honored
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_time_honored
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_time_honored
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_cor_time_honored
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_time_honored } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1150 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1154
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_ai_tianming.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = ai_tianming_civic
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_ai_tianming } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_ai_tianming
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_ai_tianming }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ai_tianming } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ai_tianming
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_ai_tianming
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_ai_tianming
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = ai_tianming_civic

		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_leader_lost
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_play_leader_lost } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1155 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1156
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_ruler_herta.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			always = no
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_ruler_herta } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_ruler_herta
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_ruler_herta }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ruler_herta } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ruler_herta
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_ruler_herta
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_ruler_herta
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
