			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_herta_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_herta_2 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_herta_2 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_herta_2
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_herta_2
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_herta_2
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_herta_2
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_herta_2 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_herta_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_herta_2 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_herta_2 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_herta_2
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_herta_2
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_herta_2
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_herta_2
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_herta_2 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_herta_2
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_herta_2 } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1158 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1161
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_country_flag = tr_cunhu_fengrao_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_country_flag = tr_cunhu_fengrao_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_country_flag = tr_cunhu_fengrao_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_country_flag = tr_cunhu_fengrao_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_country_flag = tr_cunhu_fengrao_1
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_cunhu_fengrao_1 } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1161 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_huimie_1
		}
		if = {

				set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_huimie_1

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1164
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_cunhu_jiyi_1.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = official leader_class = scientist }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_cunhu_jiyi_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_cunhu_jiyi_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_cunhu_jiyi_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_jiyi_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_cunhu_jiyi_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_cunhu_jiyi_1

				has_country_flag = tr_cunhu_xuwu_1
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_cunhu_xuwu_1 } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1165 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1166
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_country_flag = tr_xunlie_fanyu_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_country_flag = tr_xunlie_fanyu_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_xunlie_fanyu_1

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1169
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_xunlie_jiyi_1.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			limit = {

				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_xunlie_jiyi_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_xunlie_jiyi_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_xunlie_jiyi_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_xunlie_jiyi_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_xunlie_jiyi_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1

			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}

				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_xunlie_fengrao_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_xunlie_fengrao_1
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_xunlie_fengrao_1
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_country_flag = tr_xunlie_fengrao_1
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_xunlie_fengrao_1 } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1170 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_StellaronHunter_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_StellaronHunter_1
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_StellaronHunter_1
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_StellaronHunter_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_StellaronHunter_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"

		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_authority = auth_global_giant_enterprise
				always = no
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_ruler_ipc_chairman } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1175 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1176
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_marketing_expansion_department.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_marketing_expansion_department
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_marketing_expansion_department } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_marketing_expansion_department
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_marketing_expansion_department }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_marketing_expansion_department } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_marketing_expansion_department
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_marketing_expansion_department
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_marketing_expansion_department
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_marketing_expansion_department
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_marketing_expansion_department } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_marketing_expansion_department }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_marketing_expansion_department } }

country_event = {

	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

				is_pool_leader = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_building_materials_logistics_department } }
		}

		}

			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_building_materials_logistics_department }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_building_materials_logistics_department } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_building_materials_logistics_department

		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_techniques_developing_department
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_techniques_developing_department } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1180 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1181
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_human_resources_incentive_department.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_human_resources_incentive_department
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_human_resources_incentive_department } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_human_resources_incentive_department
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_human_resources_incentive_department }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_human_resources_incentive_department } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_human_resources_incentive_department
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_human_resources_incentive_department
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_human_resources_incentive_department
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_human_resources_incentive_department
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_human_resources_incentive_department } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {

country_event = {

	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1

			limit = {

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_confidential_code_breaking_department
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_confidential_code_breaking_department } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1185 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

			}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_erxiangleyuan_1
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_civic_erxiangleyuan_1 } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_civic_erxiangleyuan_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_civic_erxiangleyuan_1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_civic_erxiangleyuan_1 } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_civic_erxiangleyuan_1
				unlock_council_slots = 1

			}

				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_destruction } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_destruction
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_oracle_destruction
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_oracle_destruction
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_The_Destruction
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_destruction } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"

				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }

		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1188 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1189
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_oracle_harmony.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no

				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}

		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1190 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1191
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_oracle_hunt.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_The_Hunt
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_hunt } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_oracle_hunt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_oracle_hunt }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_hunt } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_hunt
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_oracle_hunt
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_oracle_hunt
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_The_Hunt
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_hunt } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_oracle_hunt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

				}
			}
			if = {

				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_nihility
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_oracle_nihility
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_oracle_nihility
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_The_Nihility
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_nihility } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_oracle_nihility
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_oracle_nihility }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_nihility } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_nihility
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_oracle_nihility
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_oracle_nihility
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_The_Nihility
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_nihility } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_The_Nihility
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_nihility } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1193 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_The_Propagation
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_fanyu } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_oracle_fanyu
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_oracle_fanyu }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_fanyu } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_oracle_fanyu
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_oracle_fanyu
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_oracle_fanyu
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_The_Propagation
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_oracle_fanyu } }

				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_yunqi_commander
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_yunqi_commander
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_zhuxing
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_yunqi_commander } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_yunqi_commander
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_yunqi_commander }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_yunqi_commander } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_yunqi_commander
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_yunqi_commander
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_yunqi_commander
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_zhuxing
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_yunqi_commander } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1198 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1199
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_shiwang_si.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

		}

			set_council_position = councilor_shiwang_si
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_shiwang_si }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_shiwang_si } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_shiwang_si
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_shiwang_si
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_shiwang_si
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

			}

				set_council_position = councilor_ruler_great_guardian
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1202
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_tiewei.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}

		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			always = yes

			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_tiewei } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_tiewei }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_tiewei } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_tiewei
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_tiewei
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_tiewei
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_yaliluo_tech }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_yaliluo_tech } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_yaliluo_tech
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_yaliluo_tech
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_yaliluo_tech
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				always = yes
			has_valid_civic = civic_yaliluo_tech
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_yaliluo_tech } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1203 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			always = no
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_ruler_warshipgirls } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_ruler_warshipgirls
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_ruler_warshipgirls }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ruler_warshipgirls } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ruler_warshipgirls
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_ruler_warshipgirls
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_ruler_warshipgirls
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			always = no
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_ruler_warshipgirls } }

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				always = no
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_ruler_warshipgirls } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1206 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1212
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_wg_knight.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_wg_knight
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_wg_knight } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_wg_knight
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_wg_knight }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_knight } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_knight
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_wg_knight
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_wg_knight
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_yamato
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_yamato } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1214 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1217
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_sh_BOSSAP.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_BOSSAP
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_BOSSAP } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_sh_BOSSAP
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sh_BOSSAP }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_BOSSAP } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_BOSSAP
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_sh_BOSSAP
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sh_BOSSAP
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}

					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_sh_criminal_syndicate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate
				unlock_council_slots = 1

			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_sh_criminal_syndicate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_sh_criminal_syndicate
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_sh_criminal_syndicate } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1219 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.60 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

				OR = { leader_class = commander leader_class = official }
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_origin = origin_wg_warhammer
			has_authority = auth_warshipgirls
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_wg_war_apostles } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_wg_war_apostles
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_wg_war_apostles }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_war_apostles } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_war_apostles
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_wg_war_apostles
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_wg_war_apostles
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
